def calculate_average(numbers):
    # Check if the input is a list and not empty
    if not isinstance(numbers, list) or len(numbers) == 0:
        return 0  # Return 0 if the input is invalid

    # Calculate the sum of numbers
    total_sum = sum(numbers)

    # Calculate the average
    average = total_sum / len(numbers)

    return average

# Example usage
input_numbers = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
average = calculate_average(input_numbers)
print(f"Average of input [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]: {average}")

    [questionId] => 6
    [code] => class Stack:
    def __init__(self):
        self.items = []

    def push(self, item):
        """Add an item to the stack."""
        self.items.append(item)

    def pop(self):
        """Remove and return the top item from the stack."""
        if not self.is_empty():
            return self.items.pop()
        else:
            raise IndexError("pop from empty stack")

    def peek(self):
        """Return the top item from the stack without removing it."""
        if not self.is_empty():
            return self.items[-1]
        else:
            return None

    def is_empty(self):
        """Check if the stack is empty."""
        return len(self.items) == 0

    def size(self):
        """Return the number of items in the stack."""
        return len(self.items)

# Example usage:
if __name__ == "__main__":
    stack = Stack()
    stack.push(1)
    stack.push(2)
    stack.push(3)

    print("Stack size:", stack.size())
    print("Top element:", stack.peek())

    while not stack.is_empty():
        print("Popped:", stack.pop())

    [language] => Python
)

    [questionId] => 6
    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python
)

import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python

    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python

    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python

    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python

    [questionId] => 6
    [code] => import random
import string

class Student:
    def __init__(self, name, age, grade):
        self.name = name
        self.age = age
        self.grade = grade

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Name: {self.name}, Age: {self.age}, Grade: {self.grade}"

def generate_random_name():
    """Generate a random name."""
    letters = string.ascii_uppercase
    return ''.join(random.choice(letters) for _ in range(5))

def generate_random_students(num_students):
    """Generate a list of random students."""
    students = []
    for _ in range(num_students):
        name = generate_random_name()
        age = random.randint(18, 25)
        grade = random.randint(60, 100)
        students.append(Student(name, age, grade))
    return students

def filter_students_by_grade(students, min_grade):
    """Filter students by minimum grade."""
    return [student for student in students if student.grade >= min_grade]

def sort_students_by_grade(students):
    """Sort students by grade (descending)."""
    return sorted(students, key=lambda student: student.grade, reverse=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Generate 1000 random students
    num_students = 1000
    students = generate_random_students(num_students)

    # Filter students with grade >= 80
    filtered_students = filter_students_by_grade(students, 80)

    # Sort students by grade (descending)
    sorted_students = sort_students_by_grade(filtered_students)

    # Print the top 10 students
    print("Top 10 Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(sorted_students[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    # Calculate average age of students
    total_age = sum(student.age for student in students)
    average_age = total_age / num_students
    print(f"\nAverage Age of Students: {average_age:.2f} years")

    # Calculate average grade of students
    total_grade = sum(student.grade for student in students)
    average_grade = total_grade / num_students
    print(f"Average Grade of Students: {average_grade:.2f}")

    # Example of large output: Printing all students
    print("\nAll Students:")
    for i, student in enumerate(students, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {student}")

    [language] => Python
)

def calculate_average(numbers):
    if not numbers:
        return None
    
    total_sum = sum(numbers)
    average = total_sum / len(numbers)
    return average

# Example usage
numbers = [5, 10, 15, 20, 25]
average = calculate_average(numbers)

if average is not None:
    print(f"The average of numbers {numbers} is: {average}")
else:
    print("Unable to calculate average. List is empty.")
